# Current status

Updated: 2026-11-13. This file owns the active milestone and known limits. Code and tests remain the final evidence.

## Goal and milestones

Revised objective (owner request, 2026-11-10: app product improvement is primary; the approved 14-day production cadence is retained):

> ShortsMaker를 사용자가 앱에서 소재 선정부터 대본·장면·음성 제작, 편집·검토, 재료·예산 관리와 YouTube 비공개 업로드까지 직접 수행하고 중간 개입·재개할 수 있는 제품으로 지속 개선한다. 사용자 흐름과 실제 영상 결과에서 문제를 찾아 코드리뷰·수정·테스트·UI 검증을 반복하고 문서·커밋에 반영한다. 출처 정확성·승인 예산·재사용·복구·품질 검토를 유지하며, 14일 제작 운영을 앱과 영상 품질의 검증·피드백 과정으로 활용한다. 완성본 채택과 공개는 사용자가 결정하고 장기적으로 지속 운영·수익화를 지원한다.

### Operating and improvement cycle

- **Product-first development:** judge a change by whether a user can complete, understand, intervene in and recover the workflow through Studio. Ordinary production must progressively stop depending on developer-written scripts, internal files or a hidden agent-only procedure. Use actual videos and failures as product feedback; a CLI-rendered upload alone does not prove that the app supports the same path. Reuse the existing modular monolith and bounded services rather than building a parallel pipeline.
- Aim for one accepted private review copy per 14-day production slot with a distinct sourced topic. Keep each episode's exact script, reviewed first frames, raw clips, narration, music, final MP4, and private upload record resumable. Reuse accepted work after a code change rather than restarting paid stages.
- Owner clarification, 2026-11-03: run improvement development independently of that production cadence. Act on finished-cut feedback when received, with an automatic check **daily at 19:05 Asia/Seoul**. Select a concrete high-impact finding from feedback, retained cuts, failures or unfinished work; reproduce it, make a small complete change, verify it, update shared docs and commit. Existing-asset edits, prompt/process improvements and offline checks can proceed before the next production slot or a mature seven-day audience window. Do not invent a feature, repeat a resolved task or make a paid comparison merely to produce a daily change. New paid validation still requires a valid episode or separate approval; preserve the monthly/episode limits and accepted published assets. After each change, distinguish technical/local evidence from quality or retention validation still due on a future completed cut.
- Before each slot, verify current-period Google/OpenAI ledgers, remaining room, model estimates, a valid spending authorization, upload authentication, and a running scheduler host. The October baseline is Google ₩10,000/month and OpenAI $1/month separately; a cadence or goal edit grants no extra provider budget or reuse of a spent one-shot approval.
- Review source accuracy, story, claim-to-picture meaning, rhythm, captions, and BGM on the actual whole cut. Preserve script, first-frame, muted raw, whole-cut, and private-upload checks. Record a failed or delayed slot with its reason; stop the affected stage on exhausted/stale budget, uncertain provider/upload results, or missing approval. Retry only within the valid attempt/spend limits.
- After each finished or stopped slot, record the concrete finding and choose the highest-impact improvement needed for the next episode. Fix reproducible defects and improve the reusable pipeline; run applicable tests, update this status and any durable decision, and commit the change. Check the finding again in the next actual episode. Record “no change warranted” when the evidence supports it instead of inventing development work.
- Review published episodes over the same seven-day window when metrics exist. Missing audience data does not prevent the next production slot, but it cannot support a claim of better retention or revenue. User review remains the evidence for editorial quality; tests establish technical behavior.

### Initial product and operational acceptance

1. **App workflow:** a user can follow source discovery/selection → script and scene editing/review → first-frame/raw/narration preparation → BGM/captions/edit → whole-cut review → private YouTube upload through Studio. Regular creation and intermediate changes do not require manually editing internal files or running Python commands. Advanced/debug CLI paths can remain optional. Public release remains the owner's separate YouTube decision.
2. **Visible control and recovery:** the app shows current work, next action, prerequisites, request estimates versus remaining program budget, reusable materials and uncertain/failed requests. Saved scripts, media choices, review decisions and completed work can be reopened/reused; navigation, restart and interrupted requests cannot silently lose necessary input, repeat spending or duplicate upload. Unknown results and exclusions retain their gates.
3. **Deployed usability evidence:** relevant tests and actual UI checks cover normal, empty, failure, navigation, draft and recovery states. Verify the running revision and actual host after a safe deployment; a commit, mock UI or running process alone is insufficient. The owner can actually use and assess the deployed app. Keep unsaved-input safety while closing the current code/runtime gap.
4. **Three real app-linked operating cycles:** retain the requirement for three distinct consecutive scheduled slots with source-checked, owner-accepted private copies and exact script/video hashes and confirmed YouTube IDs. Verify the actual 14-day start/review/resume path and app controls; a manual pilot, rejected slot or developer-only script is not an accepted app cycle.
5. **Cost and quality evidence:** each real slot keeps used/rejected outputs, reservations, reconciliation and unresolved charges sufficient to check the approved limits. Preserve source, frame, raw and whole-cut review. Estimated cost, technical quality and test success do not establish factual/creative quality, audience retention or revenue.
6. **Repeated product improvement:** connect actual user/video feedback and failures to a prioritized change, code review, suitable tests/UI checks, shared handoff/commit and validation in subsequent app use. Owner usability acceptance and the product criteria above are required alongside the three operating cycles. After this initial baseline is met, keep the same operating/improvement loop; do not invent changes when no evidence warrants them.

Current implementation falls short of this acceptance: the latest pending-script revision change is not yet deployed, and episode-scoped paid-resume controls are implemented/tested but undeployed, while remaining recovery/history flows need assessment. A read-only November11 runtime check observed Studio PID1342 and queue PID1340 started at 19:28:30 KST, after c3f6464; direct-production, auth recovery and publishing-draft UI are now visible. The new revision help/action is absent there, no embedded full build ID proves every loaded module, and current unsaved-input safety remains unconfirmed for another restart. Pending-episode planning has an explicit tested app start action. Sillim and Jamsu are owner-accepted manual pilots, not scheduled accepted cycles. The queue-only host is active and the saved cadence is **14 days**, anchored to **2026-11-04 10:06 KST**; the next cadence date is **November 18 at 10:06 KST**. The first Cheonggye private copy is attached, but on November 7 the owner found its story and visual composition unsatisfactory and requested reconsidering GPT models. This is a **received needs-improvement verdict**, not a pending viewing question, accepted cycle or confirmed public release. Keep the schedule paused and the attachment/cost history intact; do not call owner acceptance merely to resume the clock. Later November 7 an initial Terra draft stopped at BGM selection; the subsequently authorized five-scene comparison is now privately uploaded, with separate owner script reassessment and all model failures preserved below. The host's real process was observed before/after a restart with its anchor preserved. The editorial/development heartbeat remains ACTIVE **daily at 19:05 KST**, with actual November 1 and 3 checks recorded; future dispatch timing and authentication remain unproven. There are **zero verified accepted scheduled cycles**. A running host, technical tests and one private upload do not establish a completed recurring series.

## Current evidence and limits

- **2026-11-13 explicit source-input recovery — verified offline/UI; deployment pending:** selected source mode/partial URL/RSS category were previously session-only and source-only work could not use the save button. Extended existing private2MiB WorkDraft with optional strict mode/URL/category input, without provider/approval/task/episode state. Explicit save supports source-only snapshots; legacy records keep current input and non-GUI saves omit the extension. A source-only restore preserves the existing applied script/review/editor/frame/result/scope, backs up meaningful changed input first, and refuses replacement during a pending RSS read or failed backup. Whole-work restore still uses normal backup/reset rules. Changed inputs clear stale RSS candidates; visible radio/URL/category/options and preview widgets refresh without initiating a fetch or planning. Removed categories remain visible but cannot fall back to broad RSS execution. Deleted draft views cannot save/restore over a newer view.

## Studio product improvement queue (updated 2026-11-13)

The daily 19:05 KST check is a product-improvement loop with actual production as validation. Pick one supported user-flow problem, reproduce, fix/review, run appropriate offline/UI checks, update shared docs and commit/push. Inspect current code and evidence first; do not reimplement completed features. More frequent development is not a new spending authorization or another production clock.

1. **Safely deliver the current app:** close the tested-code/running-Studio gap after unsaved-input safety is known; the once-at-startup source identity and explicit comparison UI are implemented for that future check; verify the actual running revision/host and user workflow. Pending deployment is not proof of completion. While deployment is unsafe, independent free development may continue without discarding inputs or repeatedly asking the same question.
2. **Close app-only workflow gaps:** assess actual authorized-episode execution/restart recovery and remaining material-selection usability. Existing services, ledger/episode scopes, uncertain requests and hash-bound reviews remain authoritative. Exact-scene material selection is implemented; generic cross-script admission is not inferred from file existence. Explicit episode-bound frame/raw/edit/private-upload through the existing scoped CLI, reviewed pending-script revision/history/current-file handoff, auth-only check/reconnect/cancel UI, pending-episode planning preview/confirmation through the existing scoped child, scheduled review status and explicit verified private-copy attachment, library image→first-frame selection and session recovery of review inputs, read-only monthly budget visibility, plain-language editing of existing scene fields, first-frame candidate generation, retained narration selection and Generation next-step/eligibility are already implemented/tested; scene insertion/removal/reordering now uses the shared explicit-apply form and snapshots; do not treat them as missing again.
3. **Make saved work and recovery usable:** evaluate actual authorized-episode restart/resume usability and remaining retained-media and authorized-episode recovery needs; free pre-planning RSS selection, source-form menu recovery and explicit source-input WorkDraft snapshots are implemented; exact-script narration selection now persists through explicit audio settings save, keeping apply/save/approval distinct and avoiding repeat provider requests. Explicit retained-run script/version preview and safe copy-open, script/frame and per-video publishing snapshot persistence, and unapplied received-writer failure recovery through existing work drafts are implemented; unsaved typing remains server-session only and older live processes cannot use undeployed controls.
4. **Use actual app results to improve quality:** act on story/claim-to-picture meaning/rhythm/BGM/captions/cost/failure evidence rather than decorative churn. Keep the 14-day validation slots and the three-cycle evidence requirement; improve free UI/code between slots and request a separately justified paid step only when existing authority is insufficient.

### Historical auditions — not the current next task

Two zero-API source candidates were checked against their research papers on 2026-10-07 for later, distinct episodes; neither is an approved script or a production order:

- [Wind-tunnel paper](https://academic.oup.com/pnasnexus/article/5/10/pgag303/8786541) and [MIT report](https://news.mit.edu/2026/pressurized-experiments-could-help-wind-farms-generate-more-power-0928): ask why a turbine facing a changing wind may need a different blade-tip speed. The scaled, pressurized wind-tunnel experiment found that the speed ratio maximizing power changes with wind misalignment; field-farm revenue is a model projection, not an observed result. The report says experiments reached 240 atmospheres while the paper says up to 220 (the paper separately gives 24 MPa as facility capacity). Omit the pressure figure from narration until reconciled. An original yaw/speed diagram could carry the visual explanation.
- [CW-Net paper](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10950-5) and [MIT report](https://news.mit.edu/2026/system-helps-humans-predict-when-self-driving-cars-will-make-mistakes-0902): ask why a test car stopped near a cyclist even though the planner lacked the cyclist input. On a private track, the perception system detected the cyclist, but the motion planner had no appropriate cyclist features; a separate built-in safety system braked. The first-round BIKE explanation stayed below 1%, and the driver later engaged self-driving from slower speeds. The published paper also reports online prediction studies and a public-road situational-awareness study using recorded events, not a measured reduction in collisions. An original road/concept diagram can separate the observed stop, missing planner input, and backup-braking path without assigning an unreported belief to the driver.

The images offered on both MIT News pages have noncommercial, no-derivatives terms, so do not reuse them in a monetized cut. Recheck the source details and visual rights at production time. The owner has rejected the three topic auditions for story interest; keep all new paid work gated on a new story verdict and a separate budget.

Both candidates have six-scene, zero-API scripts and original-diagram briefs: [CW-Net](cw_net_review_draft.json) separates perception, motion planning, and backup braking, then ends at the measured understanding/prediction result rather than a collision-prevention claim; [wind-tunnel](wind_tunnel_review_draft.json) separates measured optimal blade-tip speed from modeled farm benefits and leaves out the disputed pressure figure. An initial caption preview exposed an awkward CW-Net scene-5 split, which was rewritten as two complete phrases. Both now have local scratch auditions described above. Neither script nor its music choice is approved for publication. Final voice timing must drive any production storyboard animation.

On 2026-10-07, the current repository preflight priced each draft's six `video` scenes at $3.84 for automatic video mode or $2.88 even in hybrid mode because every scene explicitly overrides to video. Both exceed the current $1 per-video visual estimate cap, and `PAID_PRODUCTION_ENABLED` remains false. These are estimates under local rates, not provider bills or complete run costs. Use the complete-manual-clip path in `scripts/local_review.py` for a zero-API audition; do not raise the cap to auto-generate these drafts without a separately approved production budget and revised visual plan.

## Intentionally retired

| Path or approach | Replacement | Revisit only if |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Imagen 3 image generation | Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite Image through the `global` endpoint | A supported model offers measured better quality per dollar. |
| Whole-script visual cache keys | Per-scene visual identity in `outputs/clips/scenes/` | A migration proves old artifacts are worth recovering. |

## Handoff rule

When completing a stage or finding drift, update this file's date, stage state, evidence, and limits in the same commit. Keep historical rationale in `DECISIONS.md`, not here.
